Hi, doctor. Thank you so much for seeing me. I hope I'm in the right place, and I really appreciate your time. Um, I guess what brings me in is... well, I've been having a little trouble keeping my mind on tasks lately. It's probably nothing major, just normal aging maybe, but I can't seem to stay focused long enough to finish things, even when they're really important for work. Like, I do computer support, and I'll start tracing a client's network problem, and... well, ten minutes later I'll realize I've just reread the same log entries three times and I still don't know what they say. Well, my vocabulary is fine, I think. I don't really struggle to find words. But as for forgetting things... I mean, everyone forgets a little bit, right? I am 53, so maybe it's just normal wear and tear.

Well, um... I guess I might have a little bit of trouble with that lately. It's not that I don't want to finish things, I really do try to get everything done perfectly. It's just... well, at work, we recently lost our old ticketing system and the escalation calendar we used to use. I didn't realize how much I leaned on them until they were gone.

It all sort of blurs together until, well, until something is already overdue and I'm scrambling to fix it.

Well, I definitely wouldn't say I'm moving slowly. I've always had a lot of energy, which I think is a good thing, right? It helps me get through the day. 

But, um... I guess maybe I have been a little bit... restless? It's really nothing major, just sort of nervous energy, I suppose. Oh, no, definitely not. I mean, my memory for people and places is perfectly fine. I'd never forget a client's face or anything like that. And I'm definitely not confused about where I am or what's going on.

========== OUTPUT [patient] ==========
Well, I try really hard not to be impulsive in a bad way. I always want to be thoughtful and professional. But, um... I guess maybe sometimes, especially in tech discussions at work, I can get a little... overeager? 

Like, a client will be explaining an issue they're having, and the answer just immediately jumps into my head. And... well, sometimes I end up cutting people off. Even when I know better. I might start talking over them, or I'll just sort of reach out and grab the keyboard before they've even finished explaining the problem to me. 

I feel awful when I realize I've done it, because I really don't mean to be rude or interrupt them. I just get so focused on wanting to fix it for them right then and there so they can get back to their day, you know? I'm really trying to be better about waiting until they're completely done speaking. 

Does... does that answer what you were asking? I hope that doesn't make me sound like a terrible listener or anything. I really do care about what they're saying.

Well, um... I guess if I really think back to when I was a kid... maybe a little bit? I mean, I was a good kid, I promise. I wasn't trying to be disruptive or anything. I just... I had a lot of energy. 

Actually, my mom still jokes about it sometimes. She says that back in grade school, my report cards would always say I was a bright student. But... well, they also usually mentioned that I had a hard time staying in my seat. And, um, I guess I had a habit of blurting out answers before the teacher called on me. Kind of like what I was saying about work earlier, just getting a little too eager to help. 

Oh, and the homework thing... I would actually do my homework, I swear I did! But somehow I'd manage to lose it between my house and the classroom. It was so frustrating because I really wanted to show the teacher I'd done the work. 

I hope that doesn't make me sound like I was a troublemaker. I really did want to do well and be a good student. Does that... is that the kind of thing you're looking for about my childhood?
